| `muted`, `mutedForeground` | `--muted`, `--muted-foreground` | Quiet fills, the chat user bubble, code blocks, captions | `#ededed` / `#1c1c1c`, `#666666` / `#999999` |
| `accent`, `accentForeground` | `--accent`, `--accent-foreground` | Hovered rows | `#f4f4f4` / `#141414` |
| `destructive`, `destructiveForeground` | `--destructive`, `--destructive-foreground` | Errors, destructive buttons | `#e7000b` / `#ff6467` |
| `border` | `--border` | Every hairline and default border color | `#e2e2e2` / `#262626` |
| `input` | `--input` | Input fills | `#ededed` / `#1c1c1c` |
| `ring`, `focusRing` | `--ring`, `--focus-ring` | Focus rings, the focused composer; card focus ring | `#0099ff`; `focusRing` follows `ring` |
| `hover`, `active` | `--hover`, `--active` | Translucent hover and pressed tints | ink 4.5% / 7.5%, white 6% / 10% |
| `selection`, `selectionForeground` | `--selection`, `--selection-foreground` | Text selection | `primary` at 72% |
| `sidebar`, `sidebarForeground`, `sidebarPrimary`, `sidebarPrimaryForeground`, `sidebarAccent`, `sidebarAccentForeground`, `sidebarRow`, `sidebarRowControl`, `sidebarBorder`, `sidebarRing` | `--sidebar-*` | The sidebar. `sidebar` also fills `Dialog`, `AlertDialog`, `Modal` and `Sheet`. | Kortix surface values |
| `blue` | `--kortix-blue` | The accent: a checked `Switch`, `Checkbox` or `RadioGroup`, the `brand` and `blue` `Button` variants, info `Badge`, `InfoBanner` | `#2b91f7` |
| `green`, `orange`, `yellow`, `red`, `purple` | `--kortix-*` | Status tones. Keep their meaning. | `#199338`, `#d18b19`, `#cca300`, `#f14b4c`, `#ab80d6` |
| `brand` | `--kortix-base` | The Kortix base accent | follows `ring` |
| `chart1` … `chart5` | `--chart-1` … `--chart-5` | Chart series | an amber ramp |
| `terminalSurface`, `terminalForeground`, `terminalBorder` | `--terminal-*` | The terminal pane (always dark) | `#0f0f0f`, `#e5e5e5`, white 10% |

These keys apply in both modes:

| Key | Variable | Controls | Default |
| --- | --- | --- | --- |
| `radius` | `--radius` | Every corner. `rounded` is −6px, `-sm` −4px, `-md` −2px, `-lg` equal, `-xl` +4px, `-2xl` +6px. | `0.625rem` |
| `borderWidth` | `--border-width` | Every `border*` and `divide-*` line with no explicit width | `1px` |
| `spacing` | `--spacing` | Density: every `p-*`, `m-*`, `gap-*`, `size-*` is a multiple | `0.23rem` |
| `fonts.sans`, `fonts.mono` | `--font-family-sans`, `--font-family-mono` | Body text and headings; code, ids, `Badge` | system stacks |
| `text.xs` … `text['8xl']` | `--text-<step>` | The font size of each `text-*` step | `0.8125rem` … `6rem` |
| `lineHeights.<step>` | `--text-<step>--line-height` | The line height of each `text-*` step | the Kortix ratio per step |
| `shadows['2xs']` … `shadows['2xl']` | `--shadow-<step>` | The elevation of each `shadow-*` step. `0 0 #0000` flattens one. | the Tailwind shadow ramp |
| `durations.fast` … `durations.slower` | `--duration-<step>` | `duration-fast` … `duration-slower` | `100ms`, `150ms`, `200ms`, `300ms`, `500ms` |
| `easing.default`, `in`, `out`, `inOut` | `--ease-*` | The `ease-*` curves | the Kortix curves |
| `vars` | any `--name` | Any other variable, for example `--liquid-glass-blur` | none |

#### Limits

- An explicit width class (`border-2`, `border-[3px]`) stays fixed. Only bare `border*` and `divide-*` follow `borderWidth`.
- Font sizes with no step stay fixed: the 10px, 11px and 12px captions and the `0.8rem` inline code chip.
- Durations with no step stay fixed: `duration-80`, `duration-0`, the sidebar slide (`200` / `240` / `260ms` on `cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)`), the command palette (`140` / `180ms`) and the enter and exit keyframes (`200` / `250ms`). Every `duration-100` / `150` / `200` / `300` / `500` reads `duration-fast` … `duration-slower`.
- Chat surfaces have no tokens of their own. They read `muted`, `card`, `border` and `mutedForeground`.
- Decorative values stay fixed: the dialog scrims, the `inverse` `Button` variant, the `Tag` status palette, the loader and dot-matrix gradients and the chart legend swatches.

#### Rules

- Theme through tokens. Never fork or restyle a component: a forked copy stops receiving fixes.
- Pass your logo through the `useBrand` option of `UiProvider`.
- Do not load `fonts.css`. Set `fonts.sans`.
- Plain CSS works after the `@kortix/ui` import. Put light values in `:root:not(.dark)` and dark values in `.dark`. A bare `:root` rule also matches `<html class="dark">` and overrides the dark value. `themeToCss` writes these selectors for you.

### Components and modules

#### Primitives

The root `@kortix/ui` exports the primitives with their parts: `Button`, `Badge`, `Card`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `Label`, `Checkbox`, `Switch`, `Select`, `Modal`, `Hint`, `Popover`, `DropdownMenu`, `Tabs`, `FilterBar`, `Separator`, `Skeleton`, `Bubble`, `Message`, `Marker`. Every other component is one subpath, `@kortix/ui/<file>`. The live catalog at [kortix.com/design-system](https://kortix.com/design-system) renders each one in light and dark, with its usage rules.

| Need | Compose | Import |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Actions | `Button`, `ButtonGroup`, `Toggle` | `button`, `button-group`, `toggle` |
| Forms | `Field`, `Input`, `Textarea`, `Select`, `CheckboxGroup`, `RadioGroup`, `Switch`, `Slider`, `Label` | `field`, `input`, `textarea`, `select`, `checkbox-group`, `radio-group`, `switch`, `slider`, `label` |
| Search | `InputGroupSearch`, `PageSearchBar`, `Command` | `input-group`, `page-search-bar`, `command` |
| Overlays | `Modal`, `Sheet`, `Popover`, `DropdownMenu`, `Hint` | `modal`, `sheet`, `popover`, `dropdown-menu`, `hint` |
| Confirm | `ConfirmDialog`, `AlertDialog` | `confirm-dialog`, `alert-dialog` |
| Feedback | `InfoBanner`, the toast helpers, `Loading`, `Skeleton`, `Progress` | `info-banner`, `toast`, `loading`, `skeleton`, `progress` |
| Status | `Badge`, `CountBadge`, `StatusBadge` | `badge`, `count-badge`, `status` |
| Layout | `Card`, `Section`, `Tabs`, `Disclosure`, `Accordion`, `Separator`, `ScrollArea`, `Sidebar` | `card`, `section`, `tabs`, `disclosure`, `accordion`, `separator`, `scroll-area`, `sidebar` |
| Data | `Table`, `DefinitionList`, `InlineMeta`, `Empty`, `Calendar` | `table`, `definition-list`, `inline-meta`, `empty`, `calendar` |
| People | `UserAvatar`, `EntityAvatar` | `user-avatar`, `entity-avatar` |

Compose before you create:

- `Modal` for a dialog, `Hint` for a tooltip, `Loading` for a spinner, `Badge` for a chip, `ConfirmDialog` before a destructive action.
- `Dialog` and `Tooltip` are banned in feature code. The root exports them because `Modal` and `Hint` build on them, and both carry `@deprecated`.
- Never call the `toast()` function of `sonner` directly. Use the helpers in `@kortix/ui/toast`.
- Never use an icon with `animate-spin` as a spinner. Icons come from `@phosphor-icons/react`.

#### Modules

Each module has one entry. The names an entry exports are its public API, under semver. Every module needs `UiProvider` and a `QueryClientProvider` at the root.

| Entry | Holds | Minimal mount |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `@kortix/ui/workspace` | Composed project screens: `SessionWorkspace`, `ProjectHome` (the new-session composer; it creates the session and opens it), `ProjectSessionList`, `ProjectSessionsView`, `ProjectFilesView`, `ProjectSidebar`, `ShareSessionModal` | `<SessionWorkspace session={session} />`, `<ProjectHome projectId={projectId} />`; `ProjectSessionList` inside a `SidebarProvider` |
| `@kortix/ui/session` | The conversation: `SessionThread`, turns, tool rows, `ToolRegistry`, `BasicTool`, `GenericTool`, prompts, `ModelSelector`, `AgentSelector` | `<SessionThread session={session} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/composer` | The composer: `ComposerChatInput`, `SessionChatInput`, its parts and the send pipeline | `<ComposerChatInput projectId={projectId} onSend={...} />` in the project scope |
| `@kortix/ui/session-panel` | The side panel: `SessionPanelProvider`, `ActionPanel`, `SessionFiles`, `SessionChanges`, `SessionAudit`, `openSessionQuickView` | `SessionPanelProvider` with the runtime ids |
| `@kortix/ui/files` | Project files (the git-backed repo: `ProjectFilesProvider`, `FileExplorerPage`, `DriveExplorer`), sandbox files (`SandboxFileExplorer`), `FilePreviewHost`, drive and change-request hooks | `ProjectFilesView` from `workspace` (it fills its parent; the parent sets the height); `<FilePreviewHost />` once on a page with no side panel and no `SessionThread` |
| `@kortix/ui/file-viewer` | `FileViewer`, `FileContentRenderer` (pdf, docx, xlsx, csv, pptx, zip, images, code), `FilePreviewModal`, `FileSourceProvider` | `<FileViewer content={text} fileName="notes.md" />` |
| `@kortix/ui/terminal`, `@kortix/ui/browser` | `SessionTerminal`; `SessionBrowser`, `BrowserLanding` | the runtime ids of a mounted `useSession` |
| `@kortix/ui/markdown` | `UnifiedMarkdown`, `MarkdownWithFrontmatter`, `HighlightedCode` | `<UnifiedMarkdown content={text} trust="agent" />` |
| `@kortix/ui/genui` | `GenuiBlock` and `genuiComponents`, the web component set over `@kortix/sdk/genui/react`. See [Render blocks in React](/docs/developers#render-blocks-in-react). | none |
| `@kortix/ui/diff`, `@kortix/ui/changes` | `DiffView`; `ChangeList` | `<DiffView before={{ name, contents }} after={{ name, contents }} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/apps` | `AppPreview` and the App view model | none |
| `@kortix/ui/access`, `@kortix/ui/customize` | Sharing and roles (`AccessDialog`, `RoleSelect`, `PrincipalPicker`); the project configuration pages (`ConnectorsPage`, `AgentsPage`, `TriggersPage`, `SecretsPage`, `ModelsPage`, `SkillsPage`, `ProjectSettingsPage`) | `<ConnectorsPage projectId={projectId} />` |
| `@kortix/ui/theme`, `@kortix/ui/i18n`, `@kortix/ui/icons` | The typed theme; `useTranslations` and `useLocale`; the library's glyphs | none |

The package skill lists every name of every entry with its props and one example: `packages/ui/skills/kortix-ui/references/modules.md`.

<Callout type="warning">
Deep paths have no semver. `@kortix/ui/internal/*` and `@kortix/ui/<module>/<path>` (for example `@kortix/ui/session/thread/session-chat`) resolve, but they exist only for Kortix's own apps (`packages/ui/scripts/deep-exports.json`). They change or disappear in any release. Import the entry. If an entry lacks a name you need, report it so the name is added to the entry.
</Callout>

### Build a dashboard

A dashboard is your chrome around the composed screens: the Kortix session list in your sidebar, `ProjectHome` to start a session, `SessionWorkspace` to work in one, `ProjectFilesView` for the project's files. The steps below are the files of one Vite + React 19 + react-router app. Add `react-router-dom` to the install in [Install and peers](/docs/developers#install-and-peers). The browser holds a signed-in user's refreshing token, never an API key.

<Steps>
<Step title="Create the client">
Start from the client in [Create the client](/docs/developers#create-the-client). In a browser, feed `getToken` from the headless session store ([Sign users in headlessly](/docs/developers#sign-users-in-headlessly)). The store keeps the user's tokens and refreshes them 60 s before they expire.

```ts title="src/kortix.ts"
import { createKortix, createKortixSession } from '@kortix/sdk';

// The signed-in user's tokens. Memory only: pass `storage` to keep them across reloads.
export const authSession = createKortixSession();

// One client per host, at module scope. Every @kortix/sdk/react hook and
// every @kortix/ui component reads it.
export const kortix = createKortix({
  backendUrl: import.meta.env.VITE_KORTIX_API_URL ?? 'https://api.kortix.com/v1',
  getToken: () => authSession.getToken(),
});
```

The browser origin must be in the API's CORS list. In local development, proxy `/v1` through your dev server and point `backendUrl` at it. Inside a Kortix App, the client acts as the viewer instead: see [Know the viewer in a Kortix App](/docs/developers#know-the-viewer-in-a-kortix-app).
</Step>
<Step title="Connect your router and theme">
```ts title="src/theme.ts"
import type { KortixTheme } from '@kortix/ui/theme';

export const acmeTheme: KortixTheme = {
  light: { blue: '#0f766e', ring: '#0f766e', border: '#cbd5e1', muted: '#f1f5f9' },
  dark: { blue: '#2dd4bf', ring: '#2dd4bf', border: '#334155', muted: '#1e293b' },
  radius: '0.375rem',
};
```

```tsx title="src/router-adapters.tsx"
import type { UiLinkComponent, UiNavigation, UiRouter } from '@kortix/ui';
import { useMemo } from 'react';
import { Link, useLocation, useNavigate, useParams, useSearchParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export const RouterLink: UiLinkComponent = ({ href, prefetch: _prefetch, ...props }) => (
  <Link to={href} {...props} />
);

function useUiRouterAdapter(): UiRouter {
  const navigate = useNavigate();
  return useMemo(
    () => ({
      push: (href) => navigate(href),
      replace: (href) => navigate(href, { replace: true }),
      prefetch: () => {},
      back: () => navigate(-1),
    }),
    [navigate],
  );
}

export const navigation: UiNavigation = {
  useRouter: useUiRouterAdapter,
  usePathname: () => useLocation().pathname,
  useSearchParams: () => useSearchParams()[0],
  useParams: () => useParams(),
};
```

```tsx title="src/providers.tsx"
import { UiProvider } from '@kortix/ui';
import { IconProvider } from '@kortix/ui/icon-provider';
import { themeToCss } from '@kortix/ui/theme';
import { Toaster } from '@kortix/ui/toast';
import { QueryClient, QueryClientProvider } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { useState, type ReactNode } from 'react';

import './kortix';
import { navigation, RouterLink } from './router-adapters';
import { acmeTheme } from './theme';

const themeCss = themeToCss(acmeTheme);
const config = { managedModelsEnabled: true };

// Mount inside the router: `navigation` calls its hooks.
export function Providers({ children }: { children: ReactNode }) {
  const [queryClient] = useState(() => new QueryClient());
  return (
    <QueryClientProvider client={queryClient}>
      <UiProvider link={RouterLink} navigation={navigation} config={config}>
        <IconProvider>
          <style href="acme-theme" precedence="default">{themeCss}</style>
          {children}
          <Toaster />
        </IconProvider>
      </UiProvider>
    </QueryClientProvider>
  );
}
```

React 19 hoists `<style href precedence>` into `<head>`. A Next.js host passes `next/link` and the `next/navigation` hooks unchanged.
</Step>
<Step title="Put the session list in your sidebar">
```tsx title="src/project-shell.tsx"
import { SessionIdsProvider } from '@kortix/ui';
import {
  Sidebar,
  SidebarContent,
  SidebarGroup,
  SidebarHeader,
  SidebarInset,
  SidebarMenu,
  SidebarMenuButton,
  SidebarMenuItem,
  SidebarProvider,
  SidebarRail,
} from '@kortix/ui/sidebar';
import { ProjectSessionList } from '@kortix/ui/workspace';
import { FolderSimpleIcon, NavigationArrowIcon, SquaresFourIcon } from '@phosphor-icons/react';
import { Link, Outlet, useLocation, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function ProjectShell() {
  const { projectId = '', sessionId } = useParams();
  const { pathname } = useLocation();
  const nav = [
    { to: `/projects/${projectId}`, label: 'New session', icon: NavigationArrowIcon },
    { to: `/projects/${projectId}/files`, label: 'Files', icon: FolderSimpleIcon },
    { to: '/', label: 'All projects', icon: SquaresFourIcon },
  ];
  return (
    <SessionIdsProvider projectId={projectId} sessionId={sessionId}>
      <SidebarProvider>
        <Sidebar collapsible="offcanvas" variant="inset">
          <SidebarHeader className="px-3 py-2 text-sm font-medium">Acme Agents</SidebarHeader>
          <SidebarContent className="min-h-0">
            <SidebarGroup className="py-0">
              <SidebarMenu>
                {nav.map(({ to, label, icon: Icon }) => (
                  <SidebarMenuItem key={to}>
                    <SidebarMenuButton asChild isActive={pathname === to}>
                      <Link to={to}>
                        <Icon />
                        <span>{label}</span>
                      </Link>
                    </SidebarMenuButton>
                  </SidebarMenuItem>
                ))}
              </SidebarMenu>
            </SidebarGroup>
            {/* The Kortix session list: sections, status, rename, delete, live updates. */}
            <SidebarGroup className="min-h-0 flex-1 flex-col py-0">
              <ProjectSessionList projectId={projectId} />
            </SidebarGroup>
          </SidebarContent>
          <SidebarRail />
        </Sidebar>
        <SidebarInset className="h-dvh min-h-0 overflow-hidden">
          <Outlet />
        </SidebarInset>
      </SidebarProvider>
    </SessionIdsProvider>
  );
}
```
</Step>
<Step title="Add the pages">
```tsx title="src/pages.tsx"
import { useProjects, useSession } from '@kortix/sdk/react';
import { Card, CardContent, CardHeader, CardTitle } from '@kortix/ui';
import Loading from '@kortix/ui/loading';
import { ProjectFilesView, ProjectHome, SessionWorkspace } from '@kortix/ui/workspace';
import { Link, useParams } from 'react-router-dom';

export function ProjectsPage() {
  const projects = useProjects();
  return (
    <main className="mx-auto flex max-w-xl flex-col gap-4 p-8">
      <Card>
        <CardHeader>
          <CardTitle>Projects</CardTitle>
        </CardHeader>
        <CardContent className="flex flex-col gap-1">
          {projects.isLoading && <Loading />}
          {projects.data?.map((project) => (
            <Link
              key={project.project_id}
              to={`/projects/${project.project_id}`}
              className="hover:bg-accent rounded-md px-2 py-1.5"
            >
              {project.name}
            </Link>
          ))}
        </CardContent>
      </Card>
    </main>
  );
}

// The project home: sending creates the session and opens it.
export function HomePage() {
  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();
  return <ProjectHome projectId={projectId} />;
}

// The session: the thread and the side panel (files, viewer, terminal, browser).
export function SessionPage() {
  const { projectId = '', sessionId = '' } = useParams();
  const session = useSession(projectId, sessionId);
  return <SessionWorkspace key={sessionId} session={session} />;
}

// The project's git-backed files. It fills its parent.
export function FilesPage() {
  const { projectId = '' } = useParams();
  return <ProjectFilesView projectId={projectId} />;
}
```

`ProjectHome` takes `beforeSend` to gate a send and `onSend` to replace it.
</Step>
<Step title="Sign in and route">
```tsx title="src/main.tsx"
import { Button, Input, Label, setUiRoutes } from '@kortix/ui';
import { StrictMode, useEffect, useState } from 'react';
import { createRoot } from 'react-dom/client';
import { createBrowserRouter, Outlet, RouterProvider } from 'react-router-dom';

import './app.css';
import './shell-row';
import { authSession, kortix } from './kortix';
import { FilesPage, HomePage, ProjectsPage, SessionPage } from './pages';
import { ProjectShell } from './project-shell';
import { Providers } from './providers';

function SignIn() {
  const [signedIn, setSignedIn] = useState(authSession.current() !== null);
  const [email, setEmail] = useState('');
  const [password, setPassword] = useState('');
  const [error, setError] = useState<string | null>(null);

  useEffect(() => authSession.subscribe((session) => setSignedIn(session !== null)), []);

  if (signedIn) return <Outlet />;
  return (
    <form
      className="mx-auto flex max-w-sm flex-col gap-3 p-8"
      onSubmit={async (event) => {
        event.preventDefault();
        setError(null);
        try {
          const { session, user } = await kortix.auth.signInWithPassword({ email, password });
          await authSession.set(session, user);
        } catch (err) {
          setError(err instanceof Error ? err.message : 'Sign-in failed');
        }
      }}
    >
      <Label htmlFor="email">Email</Label>
      <Input id="email" type="email" value={email} onChange={(event) => setEmail(event.target.value)} />
      <Label htmlFor="password">Password</Label>
      <Input id="password" type="password" value={password} onChange={(event) => setPassword(event.target.value)} />
      {error && <p className="text-destructive text-sm">{error}</p>}
      <Button type="submit" disabled={!email || !password}>
        Sign in
      </Button>
    </form>
  );
}

// This app serves `project`, `files` and `session` at their default paths.
// Every link to another page is hidden.
setUiRoutes({
  sessions: () => null,
  drive: () => null,
  apps: () => null,
  members: () => null,
  sandboxSettings: () => null,
  customize: () => null,
});

const router = createBrowserRouter([
  {
    element: (
      <Providers>
        <SignIn />
      </Providers>
    ),
    children: [
      { path: '/', element: <ProjectsPage /> },
      {
        path: '/projects/:projectId',
        element: <ProjectShell />,
        children: [
          { index: true, element: <HomePage /> },
          { path: 'files', element: <FilesPage /> },
          { path: 'sessions/:sessionId', element: <SessionPage /> },
        ],
      },
    ],
  },
]);

createRoot(document.getElementById('root')!).render(
  <StrictMode>
    <RouterProvider router={router} />
  </StrictMode>,
);
```

`./app.css` holds the imports from [Add the styles](/docs/developers#add-the-styles). `./shell-row` is the row from [Replace one tool row](/docs/developers#replace-one-tool-row).
</Step>
</Steps>

- More data comes from the hooks in [React hooks](/docs/developers#react-hooks), for example `useProjectSessions`, `useProjectApps` and `useProjectTriggers`.
- Never `fetch` the Kortix API yourself. If the SDK lacks a call you need, report it as an SDK gap.
- Three white-label examples follow this structure ([overview](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label)):
  - [Lumen Workspace](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/reskin): the Kortix workspace under its own theme, with direct mode (the browser holds the credential) or wrapper mode (its own server holds it).
  - [Team Console](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/team-console): a static [Kortix App](/docs/guide#apps) over one account, signed in as the viewer.
  - [Lumen](https://github.com/kortix-ai/suna/tree/dev/apps/white-label/lumen): a product with its own users, data and billing, with Kortix as the agent backend.

### For agents

`@kortix/ui` ships the `kortix-ui` skill at `packages/ui/skills/kortix-ui/SKILL.md`. Every Kortix project gets it as a managed skill. An agent loads it when its `skills` grant in `kortix.yaml` is `all` or names `kortix-ui`. It tells an agent how to set up the package, which component to compose, which primitives are banned and how to verify a page. Its references:

| File | Holds |
| --- | --- |
| `references/custom-dashboard.md` | The dashboard of this section as one checklist: install, client, router adapters, theme, sidebar, pages, a custom tool row, an App's sign-in, verify. |
| `references/modules.md` | Every name of every entry, with its props and an example. |
| `references/theming.md` | Every theme key. |

In a Kortix session, an agent reads a reference with `kortix system-skills file kortix-ui references/custom-dashboard.md`. Point your own coding agent at `SKILL.md` and its references before it writes UI with `@kortix/ui`.

## SDK reference

This section is for lookup. Each table gives the name, the arguments, what the call returns and the errors it adds. The guides in [SDK](/docs/developers#sdk), [Sessions from code](/docs/developers#sessions-from-code), [Projects from code](/docs/developers#projects-from-code) and [React](/docs/developers#react) show each call in use.

Conventions in this section:

- `kortix` is the client from `createKortix`. `p` is `kortix.project(projectId)`. `s` is `kortix.session(projectId, sessionId)`.
- Route paths are relative to `backendUrl`, which ends in `/v1`. The [REST reference](/docs/developers#rest-api) lists each route.
- A failed call throws `ApiError`. `status` is the HTTP status, `code` is the API's error code, `details` is the parsed response body and `detail` is the FastAPI error field. The error catalog is in [Sessions from code > Errors and retries](/docs/developers#errors-and-retries).
- Creating a handle (`kortix.project(id)`, `kortix.session(pid, sid)`, `p.connector(slug)`) sends no request.

### Entry points and stability

| Import | Status | What it holds |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `@kortix/sdk` | Stable | The client and every framework-free module. It imports no React and no `node:*` module. It runs in browsers, Node 18 or later, Bun and edge runtimes. |
| `@kortix/sdk/react` | Stable | The React hooks. It is the only entry that imports React. Peers: `react`, `@tanstack/react-query`. |
| `@kortix/sdk/server` | Stable | Server helpers for Node and Bun (table below). It imports `node:async_hooks`. Never bundle it for a browser. |
| `@kortix/sdk/genui` | Stable | The Generative UI parser and text renderers. Peers: `zod`, `@openuidev/lang-core@0.3.1`. See [Generative UI](/docs/developers#generative-ui). |
| `@kortix/sdk/genui/fence` | Stable | `splitGenui`, `separateGenuiClosers`, `genuiVersionOf`, `genuiVersionFromClassName`. It loads no `zod`. |
| `@kortix/sdk/genui/react` | Stable | `GenuiBlock`, `useGenuiParse`, `defaultGenuiComponents`. |
| `@kortix/sdk/wire-message-id` | Stable | `mintWireMessageId` and `WIRE_MESSAGE_ID` in one module with no imports. The root exports the same names. |
| `@kortix/sdk/workspace-search` | Stable | `createWorkspaceSearchClient` and its ranking helpers (see [Workspace search](#workspace-search)). |
| `@kortix/sdk/capture-rules` | Stable | The Kortix Capture constants: `CAPTURE_LAYERS`, `CAPTURE_EXCLUSION_GROUPS`, `CAPTURE_RETENTION_LIMITS`, `CAPTURE_FRAME_INTERVALS_MS`, `CAPTURE_MAX_HEIGHTS`, `CAPTURE_DEVICE_DEFAULTS`, `normalizeCaptureAppId`, `normalizeCaptureDomain`. |
| `@kortix/sdk/internal/*` | Internal | State stores of the Kortix web app, for example `sync-store`, `server-store`, `sandbox-connection-store`, `idb-sync-cache`, `diagnostics-store`, `managed-storage`. No semver. Do not import. The first three are also deprecated. |
| Other subpaths | Deprecated | Aliases that resolve until the next major. See [Deprecated names](#deprecated-names). |

Stable entry points follow semver. A deep path into `dist/` has no semver.

#### `@kortix/sdk/server`

| Export | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `createScopedKortix(config)` | A client like `createKortix`, but every call, including calls on its `project()` and `session()` handles, uses this config only. Create one per request. See [Serve many users from one backend](/docs/developers#serve-many-users-from-one-backend). |
| `runWithKortix(config, fn)` | Runs `fn` with a request-scoped config (Node `AsyncLocalStorage`). |
| `getScopedConfig()` | The config in scope, or `undefined` outside `runWithKortix`. |
| `forwardKortixRequest(options)` | Forwards one same-origin request to the Kortix API with an upstream token. It strips host credentials and keeps a streaming response body. An upstream failure returns `502` with `{ error: 'Upstream request failed' }`. |
| `createKortixAuth(options)` | Sign in with Kortix for your own app: OAuth 2.1 with PKCE, an encrypted session cookie, refresh, sign-out, `/me` and a same-origin `/proxy`. See [Sign in with Kortix](/docs/developers#sign-in-with-kortix). |
| `readAppViewer(request, options?)` | Inside a Kortix App: verifies the signed viewer header and returns the viewer, or `null`. |
| `createAppViewerKortix(request, options)` | Inside a Kortix App: a client that calls the Kortix API as the viewer. |
| `createKortixAppGuard(options)` | One viewer resolver for an App. `requireViewer` and `requireGroup` return a viewer or a `Response`. See [Know the viewer in a Kortix App](/docs/developers#know-the-viewer-in-a-kortix-app). |
| `KortixAuthError`, `AppViewerUnavailableError` | Errors of `createKortixAuth` and of the App viewer helpers. |
| `safeReturnTo` | Accepts a same-origin return path and rejects every other value. |
| `APP_VIEWER_HEADER`, `APP_VIEWER_TOKEN_HEADER`, `APP_VIEWER_SECRET_ENV`, `KORTIX_SESSION_SENTINEL` | Header and environment variable names. |

### The client

`createKortix(config)` returns the client. Its options are in [Create the client](/docs/developers#create-the-client).

| Member | What it reaches | Routes |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `config` | The config in effect. | none |
| `auth` | Headless sign-up, sign-in, magic link, OTP, OAuth, SSO, MFA, refresh, sign-out, and the token store `auth.session(options)`. | `/auth/…` |
| `accounts` | Accounts, members, invites, API keys, connected apps, account secrets, branding, audit log. | `/accounts/…` |
| `iam` | Assignments, roles, permissions, groups, agent identities, OAuth clients, permission probes. | `/accounts/{accountId}/iam/…` |
| `accountInvites` | The invitee's side of an account invite. | `/account-invites/…` |
| `projects` | Project list, create, provision, model access and custom model providers. | `/projects/…` |
| `project(projectId)` | The [project handle](#the-project-handle). | `/projects/{projectId}/…` |
| `session(projectId, sessionId)` | The [session handle](#the-session-handle). | `/projects/{projectId}/sessions/{sessionId}/…` |
| `connectors` · `connector(slug)` | Connector calls with a token that carries its project, such as a session token. Same methods as the data-plane half of `p.connectors` and as `p.connector(slug)`. | `/connectors/…` |
| `github` | GitHub App installations and repository links, per account. | `/projects/github/…` |
| `gitBackend` | The instance git backend that holds Kortix-managed repositories. | `/projects/git/backend` |
| `billing` | Plan, credit balance, transactions, usage, per-session usage, subscription. | `/billing/…` |
| `sandboxShares` | Public share links for one sandbox port. | `/p/share` |
| `connectStatus()` | Whether the managed OAuth connect provider is configured on this instance. | `/connectors/connect-status` |
| `marketplace` | The public marketplace catalog and its sources. | `/marketplace/…` |
| `capture` | Kortix Capture for one account, the device sign-in approval, and the caller's own reads. | `/accounts/{accountId}/capture/…`, `/capture/…` |
| `notifications` | The push tokens of a person's native devices. | `/notifications/…` |
| `mobileClientPolicy()` | `{ minimum_version, store_url_ios, store_url_android }`. A `null` field means the operator set no value. Needs no token. | `/clients/mobile` |
| `validateToken()` | Checks a pasted API key. Never throws. | `/accounts/me` |
| `runtime()` | Escape hatch: the runtime client of the process-wide active sandbox. It throws on a scoped client. Use the [session handle](#the-session-handle) instead. | sandbox |

#### `kortix.projects`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `list()` · `listForAccount(accountId)` | Projects the caller can open · projects in one account. |
| `get(projectId)` · `detail(projectId)` | The summary · the full detail with config. |
| `provision(input)` | A new project on a new Kortix-managed repository. Input: `{ name, account_id?, seed_starter?, starter_template?: 'general-knowledge-worker' \| 'minimal', marketplace_items?, source_item_id?, default_branch?, icon?, icon_glyph?, idempotency_key? }`. |
| `create(input)` · `createRepo(input)` | A project on an existing `repo_url` · a project on a new GitHub repository. |
| `update(projectId, input)` · `archive(projectId)` | Change settings · archive. |
| `llmCatalog(projectId)` · `modelPicker(projectId)` | The full model catalog · the compact list for a model picker. |
| `modelAccess(projectId)` · `setModelAccess(projectId, { target: 'provider' \| 'model', id, enabled })` | Read · turn one provider or model on or off. Provider id `kortix` is the Kortix-managed models. Disabling the current default returns `409 cannot_disable_default`. |
| `customLlmProviders(projectId)` | Every custom OpenAI-compatible provider of the project, as `CustomLlmProvider[]`. A legacy key-only row has `configured: false`. |
| `setCustomLlmProvider(projectId, providerId, { name, base_url, models })` | Create or replace one custom provider. Each `models` entry is `{ id, name, context?, output?, attachment?, reasoning? }`; a field you leave out comes from the endpoint's `GET /models`. Rejects with `400` `invalid_body`, `reserved_provider_id` or `unsafe_base_url`. |
| `deleteCustomLlmProvider(projectId, providerId)` | Delete the definition, then its `CUSTOM_<ID>_API_KEY` project secret. A missing definition or key is not an error. |
| `sandboxHealth(projectId)` · `sandboxTemplates(projectId)` | Sandbox build health · build templates. |
| `sessions(projectId)` · `createSession(projectId, input?)` | Same as `p.sessions.list()` · `p.sessions.create()`. |

`idempotency_key` is 1 to 200 characters of `A–Z a–z 0–9 . _ : -`. Send one new value per create attempt. A repeat with the same key returns the first project (`201`, same `project_id`). A repeat while the first call runs returns `409 provision_in_flight`: retry with the same key.

#### `kortix.github` and `kortix.gitBackend`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `github.getInstallation(accountId)` · `github.listInstallations(accountId)` | The account's GitHub App installation · the installations the user can reach. |
| `github.listLinkableInstallations(input)` · `.linkInstallation(input)` · `.saveInstallation(input)` · `.deleteInstallation(accountId, installationId?)` | Installations the GitHub user may link · link · record · unlink. |
| `github.listRepositories(accountId, installationId?)` · `.listRepositoryBranches(…)` | Repositories the installation sees · their branches and default branch. |
| `github.linkRepository(input)` | Import a repository as a project. |
| `github.replaceProjectRepository(input)` | Point a project at another repository. A stale `expected_repo_url` returns `409`. New sessions use the new repository. Running sessions keep their checkout. |
| `github.storeUserToken(input)` | Store the user's GitHub authorization. It is necessary to create a repository in a personal GitHub account. |
| `gitBackend.get()` | `{ configured, kind: 'app' \| 'pat' \| null, owner }`. Any signed-in user can read it. |
| `gitBackend.repositories({ search?, limit? })` | Repositories under that owner. Self-host operators only; other callers get `403`. |

#### `kortix.marketplace` and `kortix.sandboxShares`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `marketplace.items(options?)` · `.item(id)` · `.itemFile(id, path)` | Browse the catalog · one item · one file of an item. |
| `marketplace.marketplaces()` · `.featured()` | All marketplaces · featured marketplaces. |
| `marketplace.sources.list()` · `.add(input)` · `.remove(id)` | Marketplace sources. |
| `sandboxShares.list(sandboxId)` · `.create({ sandboxId, port, ttl?, label? })` · `.revoke(sandboxId, token)` | Public share links for one sandbox port. |

### Accounts, IAM, billing and notifications

#### `kortix.auth`

| Member | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `signUp` · `signInWithPassword` · `sendMagicLink` · `verifyOtp` · `signInWithProvider` · `exchangeCode` · `signInWithSso` | Sign-in flows. |
| `refresh` · `resetPassword` · `updatePassword` · `updateUserMetadata` · `user` · `signOut` | Token and account maintenance. |
| `mfa.enroll` · `mfa.challenge` · `mfa.verify` · `mfa.unenroll` | Multi-factor authentication. |
| `session(options)` | A token store. It saves tokens through your storage adapter and refreshes them 60 seconds before they expire. Pass its `getToken` to `createKortix`. |

Arguments, `HeadlessAuthError` codes and limits are in [Sign users in headlessly](/docs/developers#sign-users-in-headlessly).

#### `kortix.accounts`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `list()` · `get(accountId)` · `create({ name })` · `updateName(accountId, name)` · `leave(accountId)` | The accounts the caller belongs to. |
| `members(accountId)` · `invite(accountId, input)` · `updateMemberRole(accountId, userId, role)` · `removeMember(accountId, userId)` | Members and their account role. |
| `invites(accountId)` · `cancelInvite(accountId, inviteId)` · `resendInvite(accountId, inviteId)` | Pending invites. |
| `tokens.list(accountId?, { mine? })` · `tokens.create({ accountId?, name, expiresAt?, projectId? })` · `tokens.revoke(tokenId, accountId?)` | Personal access keys (`kortix_pat_…`). `mine: true` lists only the caller's keys. `projectId` binds the key to one project. |
| `connectedApps.list()` · `connectedApps.revoke(…)` | OAuth and MCP clients the person approved, across all accounts. Route `/oauth/grants`. |
| `secretResources.list` · `.create` · `.rotate` · `.retry` · `.remove` · `.grant` · `.revoke` · `.setAccess` | Account-level provider secrets and the projects that may use them. |
| `branding.get(accountId)` · `.update(accountId, { app_name })` · `.uploadAsset(accountId, kind, file)` · `.removeAsset(accountId, kind)` · `.reset(accountId)` | Organization branding. `kind`: `logo`, `icon`, `favicon`, `logo_dark`, `icon_dark`, `favicon_dark`. |
| `audit.log(accountId, filters?)` · `audit.export(accountId, { format: 'csv' \| 'jsonl', …filters })` | The audit log, filtered by project, session, actor, source, outcome, request, resource, action or time · the same events as a file. |
| `audit.activity(accountId, { since?, until?, projectId?, actor?, client?, actorType?, credentialKind? })` | Activity per UTC day: per user, the active days, distinct active hours and events. `client: 'browser'` keeps only a person in a web page. |
| `audit.webhooks.list` · `.create` · `.update` · `.remove` | Signed webhooks that stream audit events to a SIEM. |

#### `kortix.iam`

An assignment is the one grant record: one principal (`user`, `group`, `service_account`, `pending` or `project`), one role, one scope (`account` or one `project`), an optional object (`agent`, `skill`, `secret`, `app`, `trigger` or `connection`) and an optional `expiresAt`.

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `assignments.list(accountId, filter?)` | Live assignments. Filters: `principalType` with `principalId`, `scopeType`, `scopeId`, `objectType`, `objectId`, `roleId`, `includeExpired`. |
| `assignments.create(accountId, { principal, scope, object?, expiresAt?, roleId \| roleKey })` · `assignments.revoke(accountId, assignmentId)` | Grant · revoke. `roleKey` names a built-in role: `owner`, `admin`, `member`, `manager`, `agent-user`. |
| `permissions.list(accountId)` · `permissions.forRole(…)` | The permission catalog (`action`, `scope_type`, `resource_type`, `delegable`, `description`, `area`, `level`, `implies`) · one role's permissions. |
| `roles.list` · `.create` · `.update` · `.setPermissions` · `.remove` · `.usage` | Custom roles. Without the account's `rbac` entitlement the API returns `402 entitlement_required`. |
| `groups.list` · `.get` · `.create` · `.update` · `.remove` · `groups.members.list` · `.add` · `.remove` | Groups and their members. |
| `agentIdentities(accountId)` | Agent principals, to bind a role to an agent. |
| `oauthClients.list` · `.get` · `.create` · `.update` · `.rotateSecret` · `.remove` | Sign in with Kortix clients. The API returns the secret once, on `create` and on `rotateSecret`. |
| `can(accountId, userId, { action, resourceType?, resourceId? })` · `canBatch(accountId, userId, probes)` | Whether a principal may do one action · many actions in one request, answers in input order. An unknown action returns `400`. |

Probe helpers (root exports):

| Name | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `PROJECT_ACTIONS` | Every project action the API answers about. Example: `PROJECT_ACTIONS.PROJECT_GITOPS_MERGE` is `project.gitops.merge`. |
| `TRIGGER_CONTROL_ACTIONS` · `triggerControlsFrom(cans)` | The trigger probes · `{ canCreate, canFire, canUpdate, canDelete }`. Create, update and delete also need `project.write`. |
| `REVIEW_CONTROL_ACTIONS` · `changeRequestControlsFrom(cans)` | The review probes · `{ canOpen, canMerge, canClose }`. Open needs `project.write` and `project.gitops.push`. Merge needs `project.write` and `project.gitops.merge`. Close and reopen need `project.write`. |
| `reviewVerdictAllowed(item, verdict, cans)` | Whether to offer one verdict on one review item. Approving a change request merges it, so it follows `canMerge`. Other verdicts need `project.write` and `project.review.act`. |

A connector call carries `can_resolve` (on a `SessionAuditAction`) or `canResolve` (on a review item). Show Approve and Deny only when it is not `false`. `resolvers` is `managers` for a session that an API token or a service account started, else `managers_and_launcher`.

#### `kortix.accountInvites`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `listMine()` | Pending invites addressed to the caller's email. |
| `describe(inviteId)` · `accept(inviteId)` · `decline(inviteId)` | Act on one invite. They need no account membership. |
| `acceptAndLand(inviteId, invite?)` | Accept and return `{ accepted, landing }`. `landing` is `{ kind: 'project', projectId, accountId }` for the first project the invite granted, or `{ kind: 'account', accountId }`. Pass the invite you read: an accept that grants nothing then lands in the first project the invite names. |

`accountInviteLanding(accepted, invite?)` (root export) applies the same rule to an invite accepted earlier, with no request.

#### `kortix.billing`

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `accountState(accountId?)` · `accountStateMinimal(accountId?)` | Plan, credit balance and limits · the short form. |
| `creditBreakdown(accountId?)` | The credit balance per bucket. |
| `transactions(params?)` · `transactionsSummary(params?)` | Transaction history · totals. |
| `usageHistory(params?)` · `usageRollup(params?)` | Usage over time · usage grouped by model, provider or day. |
| `sessionCosts.list({ accountId?, projectId?, limit?, offset? })` · `sessionCosts.get(sessionId, options?)` | Usage records per session · one session's model and compute ledger. |
| `tierConfigurations()` | The named plan tiers and the allowed range of `monthlyUsd`. |
| `setPlan({ monthlyUsd, successUrl, cancelUrl, locale?, accountId? })` | Subscribe to, or change, the credits plan. Resolves to `{ status: 'checkout_created', checkout_url }` (redirect there), `{ status: 'updated' \| 'unchanged' }`, or `{ status: 'payment_pending', client_secret }`. React: `useSetBillingPlan()`. |
| `previewPlan({ monthlyUsd, accountId? })` | What `setPlan` would do, with no change. |
| `acceptEnterpriseOffer(…)` | A Stripe Checkout link for the open Enterprise offer. Needs `billing.write`. `404` when no offer is open, `409` when the offer is invoiced. |
| `contactSales(…)` | Sends a contact request to Kortix sales. |
| `subscription.createPortalSession(returnUrl, accountId?)` · `.cancel(feedback?, accountId?)` · `.reactivate(accountId?)` | Manage the subscription. |
| `credits.purchase(input)` · `credits.autoTopupSettings(accountId?)` · `credits.configureAutoTopup(input)` | Buy credits · read and set auto top-up. |

`canOfferTopup(state)` and `canManageBilling(state)` (root exports) read an account state. `canOfferTopup` is `true` only when the plan may buy credits and the caller may change billing. Both return `false` while `state` is `undefined` or `null`. Retired billing calls are in [Deprecated names](#deprecated-names).

#### `kortix.notifications`

Every method except `releaseDeviceToken` needs a person's credential. A service account gets `403`.

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `registerDeviceToken(input)` · `unregisterDeviceToken(token)` · `releaseDeviceToken(token, { backendUrl? })` | A native app's push token. `releaseDeviceToken` needs no credential. |

A session push goes to the phones of the person who created the session. It goes out when a turn ends or fails, or when the agent asks a question or for a permission. While one of that person's tabs holds a presence lease on the session (`s.presence`), no push goes out.

#### `kortix.capture`

Capture belongs to the account. `kortix.capture.account(accountId)` binds one account. Every account call returns `403 capture_disabled` while Capture is off for the account. Reading another member's data needs the `capture.read_all` permission and writes a `capture.member_view` audit event. Writes need `capture.manage`.

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `account(id).workspace.get()` · `.workspace.setEnabled(on)` | `{ account_id, enabled, can_read_all, can_manage, updated_at }` · turn Capture on or off. |
| `account(id).devices.list({ userId?, scope? })` | Devices with live status: `recording`, `paused`, `permission_missing`, `not_recording`, or `offline` after 120 s without a status. `scope: 'account'` lists every member's devices. |
| `account(id).devices.revoke(deviceId)` · `.devices.link(deviceId, userId \| null)` · `.devices.assetUrl(deviceId, name)` | Revoke a device's token · link a device to a member, or unlink it · a 5-minute signed URL of an action screenshot. |
| `account(id).enrollment.tokens.list()` · `.tokens.create(input)` · `.tokens.revoke(tokenId)` | IT rollout tokens. |
| `account(id).enrollment.list({ status? })` · `.enrollment.approve(enrollmentId, userId?)` · `.enrollment.deny(enrollmentId)` | Computers that presented a rollout token. |
| `account(id).policy.get()` · `.policy.set(policy)` | Layers, privacy, retention, recording and notice. |
| `account(id).storage.get()` · `.storage.set(input)` | Where the account keeps recordings: the managed store or its own S3 bucket. |
| `account(id).timeline.get(query?)` · `.timeline.items(query?)` · `.timeline.days(query?)` | App and window runs for a UTC day or a window up to 31 days · frames, actions and audio in a window, 500 of each · the days with recorded items, newest first. |
| `account(id).search({ q, kinds?, app?, from?, to?, limit?, userId?, scope? })` | Full-text search over on-screen text, actions and audio, newest first. |
| `account(id).frame(frameId, { userId? })` · `.media(chunkId, { userId? })` | One frame with its on-screen text and a 5-minute signed video URL · signed URLs of an item's video and audio. |
| `deviceGrant(userCode)` · `approveDevice(userCode, accountId?)` · `denyDevice(userCode)` | The person's half of the device sign-in. |
| `me.search(query)` · `me.timeline(query)` · `me.frame(frameId)` | Reads for the person the token acts for, in the token's account. A credential that acts for no person gets `403 capture_no_human`. |

### The project handle

`kortix.project(projectId)` returns `p`. Every method has the project id bound. `p.session(sessionId)` returns the [session handle](#the-session-handle). Task guides for this handle are in [Projects from code](/docs/developers#projects-from-code).

#### Project

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `get()` · `detail()` · `update(input)` · `archive()` | Read · full detail with config · change settings · archive. |
| `llmCatalog()` · `modelPicker()` · `modelAccess()` · `setModelAccess(change)` · `customLlmProviders()` · `setCustomLlmProvider(providerId, input)` · `deleteCustomLlmProvider(providerId)` · `sandboxHealth()` | As on [`kortix.projects`](#kortixprojects), with the project id bound. |
| `validateManifest(raw)` | Validates `kortix.yaml` text on the server with the schema a merge uses. |
| `audit(options?)` | The project's audit timeline. |
| `skills.create(input)` · `skills.remove(…)` | Write a skill to the default branch · remove one. A whole-folder removal includes user edits; shared dependencies stay. |
| `attachments.upload(…)` · `.delete(…)` · `.createController(options?)` | Prompt attachments, and a controller that tracks uploads for a composer. |
| `onboardingComplete(…)` | Marks project onboarding done. |
| `updateFeatureFlag(key, enabled)` | Sets one flag for the project (`PATCH /projects/{projectId}/features`). `key` is one of `FEATURE_FLAG_KEYS`. `enabled: null` clears the override. Needs `project.settings.write`. |
| `connector(slug)` · `session(sessionId)` | One connector handle · the session handle. |

A route behind a flag that is off returns `403` with `code: 'feature_disabled'` and `feature`. Test it with `isFeatureDisabledError(error)`. Read the key with `featureDisabledKey(error)`.

#### Sessions

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `sessions.list(options?)` | One page of sessions as an array. |
| `sessions.listPage(options?)` | One page plus `next_cursor`. `null` means the last page. A cursor is valid only for the filters that issued it. |
| `sessions.create(input?)` | Creates a session. The sandbox starts in the background. Fields: [Create a session](/docs/developers#create-a-session). |

Options of `list` and `listPage`:

| Option | Values |
| --- | --- |
| `scope` | `visible` (default) or `project`: the manager inventory. Both leave out sessions the caller cannot open. |
| `limit` · `cursor` | 1 to `PROJECT_SESSION_PAGE_MAX_LIMIT` (200) rows · the `next_cursor` of the previous page. |
| `parent` | `root`: top-level sessions, each with `child_count`. A session id: that session's children. |
| `startedBy` | `me`, `others` or `automated`. |
| `createdBy` | A user id: the sessions that person started. |
| `agent` | An agent slug: the sessions that run that agent. |
| `updatedAfter` | ISO 8601: the sessions with activity at or after that time. |
| `q` | Search over every session the caller can open, 1 to 200 characters. |
| `labels` | The sessions that carry every label (exact match). |
| `view` | `pinned`, `archived`, or `deleted` (restorable sessions, each with `restore_until`). Without `view`, the list leaves out the caller's archived sessions. |

Each row carries `parent_session_id` and `initiator: { type: 'member' | 'trigger' | 'channel' | 'api' | 'system', id, label }`. `sessionParentId(session)` (root export) reads the parent.

#### Files, git and change requests

These methods read and write the project repository. To work with files in a running session, use [`s.files`](#the-session-handle).

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `files.listDirectory({ ref?, path?, cursor?, limit? })` | One folder level: `{ entries, truncated, next_cursor }`. Without `cursor` and `limit`, one response holds up to 5,000 entries. With either, a page holds `limit` entries (default 500, maximum 2,000). Pass `next_cursor` as `cursor` while it is a string. Every page reads the commit of the first page. |
| `files.list(options?)` | Every file under a path, recursive, cut at 1,000. A cut response carries `X-Kortix-Truncated: 1`. |
| `files.read(path, ref?)` · `files.history(path, { ref?, limit?, skip? })` | A file at a ref · its commits. |
| `files.write({ path, ref, content, expected_revision })` | Commits an edited text file only when its blob revision is unchanged. Returns `{ commit_sha }`. |
| `files.search(query, { content?, ref?, limit? })` | Search file names, or contents with `content: true`. A content match carries `line_number` and `line_text`. |
| `files.archive(ref, path?)` · `files.archiveRequest(ref, path?)` | Download a tarball as a `Blob` · the same download as a request your host streams to disk. |
| `git.commits()` · `git.commit(sha)` · `git.commitDiff(sha)` | The log · one commit · its diff. |
| `git.branches()` · `git.versionDiff(…)` | Branches · the diff between two refs. |
| `git.inviteCollaborator(…)` | Invites a GitHub user to a Kortix-managed repository. |
| `gitToken()` | A fresh scoped push token for a Kortix-managed repository. A bring-your-own repository returns `409`. |
| `changeRequests.list(status?, { limit?, cursor? })` · `.get(crId)` · `.diff(crId)` · `.mergePreview(crId)` | Read change requests. `list` returns one page, newest first: 50 by default, maximum 500. |
| `changeRequests.open(input)` · `.update(crId, input)` · `.merge(crId, input?)` · `.close(crId, input?)` · `.reopen(crId, input?)` | The change request lifecycle. |
| `changeRequests.requestChanges(crId, input)` | Records feedback and can send it to the session that made the change. |

Routes: `/projects/{projectId}/files`, `/commits`, `/branches`, `/version-diff`, `/change-requests`. See [Git](/docs/developers#git).

#### Review and approvals

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `review.list(params?)` | One page of the review inbox (change requests, approvals, agent outputs): `{ review_items, next_cursor }`, 50 items by default, maximum 200. Order: Needs you, Waiting, Done. Filters: `segment`, `kind`, `sessionId`. An item decided between two pages can come back on a later page: key rows by `review_item_id`. |
| `review.get(id)` · `.submit(input)` · `.act(id, …)` · `.bulkAct(input)` · `.recoverChangeRequest(…)` | One item, including `cr:<id>` and a pending `call:<id>` · submit · decide one · decide many · recover a change request. |
| `review.summary()` | `{ needs_you_total, needs_you_by_session }`, counted on the server. Use it for a badge, not the list. |
| `approvals.list(options?)` · `approvals.sessionsNeedingInput(options?)` | Connector calls that wait for a decision · sessions blocked on one, or on an open agent question, with a count per session. |
| `approvals.resolve(executionId, 'approve' \| 'deny', { note? })` | Decides one call. `note` goes to the agent as an instruction. |

#### Secrets, tokens and setup links

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `secrets.list()` | Secret metadata, never values. `status` is the shared value's state and `mine.status` the caller's own value's state. `'undecryptable'` means the server cannot decrypt it: write the value again. |
| `secrets.upsert(input)` · `secrets.remove(name)` | Create or update a write-only value with its delivery policy · delete. |
| `secrets.setStrategy(identifier, 'runtime' \| 'egress' \| 'broker' \| 'denied', options?)` | The exposure: `runtime` is an environment variable, `egress` is injected at the network edge, `broker` keeps the value out of the session, `denied` is none. |
| `secrets.broker(identifier, request)` | Sends one policy-bound HTTPS request with the secret. Needs a session-scoped token. |
| `secrets.setPersonal(name, { value?, active? })` · `secrets.removePersonal(name)` | The caller's own value, used in the caller's sessions. `active: false` keeps it but uses the shared value. |
| `secrets.setGitCredential(input)` | The credential for a bring-your-own git repository. |
| `secrets.listProviderOAuth()` · `.startProviderOAuth(…)` · `.pollProviderOAuth(…)` · `.removeProviderOAuth(provider)` | Model provider subscription logins, through a device-code flow. |
| `tokens.list()` · `tokens.create(input?)` · `tokens.revoke(tokenId)` | Project API keys. Kortix also mints one per session as `KORTIX_TOKEN`. |
| `setupLinks.requestSecret(input)` · `setupLinks.requestConnector(input)` | A link a person opens to enter a secret or connect an app, without full project access. |

#### Connectors and policies

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `connector(slug).run(action, args, options?)` | The action's output. Throws `ConnectorCallError`, or `ConnectorApprovalPendingError` while the call waits for approval. |
| `connector(slug).call` · `.describe(action?)` · `.accounts()` · `.paginate(action, args, { next, maxPages? })` · `.uploadAttachment(content, input)` | The raw result · schemas · callable accounts · a page iterator (`ConnectorPageLimitError` past `maxPages`) · stage a file. |
| `connectors.call(tool, args?, { account? })` · `connectors.callAction(slug, action, args, options?)` | Call `<connector>.<action>` · the same, typed by `kortix connectors types`. |
| `connectors.catalog(options?)` · `.tools()` · `.search(query)` · `.describe(tool)` · `.accounts(slug)` · `.uploadAttachment(…)` | Find and inspect callable tools and accounts. |
| `connectors.list()` · `.config(slug)` · `.create(input)` · `.remove(slug)` · `.sync()` · `.auth.discover(input)` | Configure connectors. `auth.discover` reads auth from an OpenAPI spec, a Postman collection or an endpoint. |
| `connectors.setName` · `.setSensitive` · `.setCredentialMode` · `.setCredential` · `.deleteCredential` · `.setOAuth2ClientCredentials` | Connector settings and the stored credential of its shared account. |
| `connectors.policies.get(slug, { policySet? })` · `.policies.set(slug, policies, { policySet? })` | One connector's base rules, or the rules of one policy set. A rule is `{ match, action, conditions? }`. |
| `connectors.policySets.list(slug)` · `.upsert(slug, name, { description? })` · `.rename(slug, name, newName)` · `.remove(slug, name)` | Policy sets: each set with its rules and the agents that use it · create one or set its description · rename it and every agent reference · delete it. `remove` returns `409 policy_set_in_use` while an agent uses the set. Root exports: `listConnectorPolicySets`, `upsertConnectorPolicySet`, `renameConnectorPolicySet`, `deleteConnectorPolicySet`. |
| `connectors.agents(…)` · `connectors.rulesMatrix()` | Every declared agent on one connector, with its grant and rule set · every connector against every agent, in one request. |
| `connectors.connections.list()` · `.listAll()` · `.reconcile(…)` · `.reconcileMember(…)` · `.updateCredential(…)` · `.revoke(…)` · `.activate(…)` · `.setDefault(…)` · `.rename(…)` · `.share(…)` · `.addComputer(…)` | Connected accounts: shared or private, defaults, labels, sharing, paired computers. |
| `connectors.discover.list` · `.sections` · `.detail` · `.tools` · `.install` | The app catalog. `list` and `sections` take `q`, `category`, `kinds` (`mcp`, `composio`, `openapi`, `graphql`) and `oneClick`. `install({ id, way?, name?, slug? })` returns `next`: `connect`, `credential` or `ready`. |
| `connectors.listMoves()` · `connectors.move(slug, { connectionId? })` | Move a Pipedream or Composio connector to its Kortix MCP app, with the same slug. `listMoves` returns `ConnectorMove[]`. `move` returns `ConnectorMoveResult`: `status: 'sign_in'` with a `connection_id`, or `status: 'moved'`. Root exports: `listConnectorMoves(projectId)`, `moveConnector(projectId, slug, { connectionId? })`. |
| `connectors.pipedream.listApps` · `.listSections` · `.connect` · `.finalize` | Legacy Pipedream connect for OAuth apps. |
| `policies.list()` · `policies.set(policies)` | Project policies. |
| `agentIdentities()` · `setAgentScope(agentName, scope)` | The project's agents as principals · bind secrets, connectors and policy sets to one agent. `scope.policy_sets` is `{ <slug>: <name> \| null }`, merged per connector; `null` or `'base'` removes the entry. |

#### Triggers and reminders

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `triggers.list()` | Every trigger, with parse errors and `triggers_paused`. |
| `triggers.create(input)` · `.update(slug, input)` · `.remove(slug)` | Write a trigger. `name` and `type` are required. `type` is `cron`, `webhook` or `app`. The API rejects `type: monitor` with `MONITOR_REMOVED` and names the replacement: an App and a `type: app` trigger. `prompt_template` is required unless `target` (`{ app, path?, method?, payload? }`) calls an App. A schedule takes `cron`, `every` or `run_at`. `type: 'app'` takes `app` and, optionally, `filter: { 'event.name': … }`. |
| `triggers.fire(slug, { payload?, dryRun? }?)` | Runs one trigger now, also while triggers are paused. `payload` deep-merges over the manual payload. `dryRun: true` writes nothing and returns `ProjectTriggerDryRunResult` (`rendered_prompt`, `filter_matches`, `session_key`, `payload`). A real fire returns `status` (`fired`, `queued` or `deduped`), `session_id`, `command_id` and `run_id`. |
| `triggers.runs(slug, { cursor?, limit? }?)` · `triggers.projectRuns({ status?, cursor?, limit? }?)` | One trigger's runs · every run of the project, newest first: `{ runs, next_cursor }`. `limit` is 1 to 100, default 25. A page can hold fewer than `limit` runs while `next_cursor` is set. |
| `triggers.replay(slug, runId, { confirmUnverified? }?)` | Fires again with the run's stored payload and the current spec. `409 run_in_progress` while the run is queued or running. `409 payload_unverified` for an unverified webhook payload without `confirmUnverified: true`. `422 payload_truncated` for a payload over 64 KiB. |
| `triggers.setActivation(paused)` | Pauses or resumes every trigger of the project. |
| `reminders.list()` · `reminders.updateMany(…)` · `reminders.removeMany(…)` | Reminders on every session the caller can open. One session's reminders are `s.reminders`. |

All trigger fields are in [Run triggers](/docs/developers#run-triggers) and [Automations](/docs/guide#automations).

#### Apps

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `apps.list()` · `.get(appId)` · `.create(input)` · `.update(appId, input)` · `.remove(appId)` | App inventory and lifecycle. |
| `apps.artifacts.register(input)` · `.uploadArchive(…)` · `.finalize(…)` | Upload a build. |
| `apps.deployments.create(appId, …)` · `.list(appId)` · `.get(…)` · `.logs(…)` · `.remove(…)` | Releases and their logs. `remove` deletes one release that is not live. |
| `apps.rollback(appId, deploymentId)` · `.start(appId)` · `.stop(appId)` | Move traffic to an earlier release · start · stop. |
| `apps.machines.list(appId)` · `.start` · `.stop` · `.restart` · `.destroy(appId, machineId)` | Machines. `destroy` keeps the machine's volume. |
| `apps.scale(appId, { <group>: count })` | Machines per process group, 0 to 20. |
| `apps.exec(appId, input)` | Runs one command in a machine. Needs `project.app.admin`. Each call is audited. |
| `apps.volumes.list(appId)` · `.create` · `.update` · `.destroy(appId, volumeId, confirmName)` · `.snapshots` · `.snapshot` · `.restore(appId, volumeId, snapshotId)` · `.fork` | Persistent volumes and snapshots. |
| `apps.access.get(appId)` · `.update(appId, …)` · `.session(appId)` · `.agents(appId)` | Who can open the App · an access session · the agents whose `kortix.yaml` grant names the App (read-only). |
| `apps.token(appId)` | A 15-minute sign-in token for the App that names the caller. Needs the `member_tokens` capability. |
| `apps.authEnv(appId)` | `KORTIX_AUTH_ISSUER`, `KORTIX_AUTH_AUDIENCE` and `KORTIX_AUTH_JWKS`, to verify App tokens with no network call. |

Routes: `/projects/{projectId}/apps/…`. See [Deploy and operate Apps](/docs/developers#deploy-and-operate-apps) and the [Apps reference](/docs/developers#apps-reference).

#### Gateway and models

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `gateway.logs(options?)` · `gateway.log(logId)` | Model request logs. |
| `gateway.overview(days?)` · `.series(days?)` · `.breakdown(days?)` · `.sessions(days?)` · `.errors(days?)` | Rollups over a window of days. |
| `gateway.budgets()` · `.setBudget(input)` · `.deleteBudget(budgetId)` | Budgets. |
| `gateway.keys()` · `.createKey(name)` · `.revokeKey(keyId)` | Gateway API keys for your own tools. |
| `gateway.routing.get()` · `.set(policy)` · `.reset()` · `.preview(input)` | The routing policy (default model, vision model, ordered fallbacks) · resolve a route with no model call. |
| `gateway.playground(prompt, models, system?)` | Runs one prompt against 1 to 6 models. |
| `modelDefaults.get()` · `.set(input)` · `.clear(params)` | Default models per account, agent and project. |
| `setDefaultAgent(agentName)` | Sets `default_agent` in `kortix.yaml`. The agent must be declared and enabled. |

See [Models](/docs/guide#models).

#### Channels

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `channels.slack.installation()` · `.mode()` · `.manifest()` · `.connect(input)` · `.completeInstall(input)` · `.disconnect()` | The project's Slack app. |
| `channels.slack.getFile(url)` · `.uploadFile(input)` | Download or upload a Slack file through the server. The bot token stays on the server. |
| `channels.teams.completeInstall(input)` | Finishes a Microsoft Teams install as the signed-in user. |

See [Channels](/docs/guide#channels).

#### Access

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `access.list()` · `access.invite(email, role, expiresAt?)` · `access.update(userId, role)` · `access.revoke(userId)` | Project members. Roles: `manager`, `member`. |
| `access.pendingInvites()` · `.resendInvite(inviteId)` · `.revokeInvite(inviteId)` | Pending invites. |
| `access.requests()` · `.approveRequest(id)` · `.rejectRequest(id)` | Access requests. |
| `access.groupGrants()` · `.attachGroupGrant(…)` · `.updateGroupGrant(…)` · `.detachGroupGrant(groupId)` | Groups with project access. |
| `access.resourceGrants.list()` · `.create(input)` · `.remove(grantId)` | Access to one object, such as one agent or skill, for a member or a group. |

See [Teams](/docs/guide#teams).

#### Sandbox

| Method | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `sandbox.list()` · `sandbox.snapshots()` | The project's sandboxes · built snapshots. |
| `sandbox.rebuildSnapshot(slug?)` · `sandbox.fixWithAgent()` | Rebuild a snapshot · ask an agent to fix a failed build. |
| `sandbox.createTemplate(input)` · `.updateTemplate(…)` · `.removeTemplate(templateId)` · `.buildTemplate(templateId)` | Sandbox templates. |
| `sandbox.setProvider(provider \| null)` | Pins the sandbox provider, or `null` for the platform default. Returns `kind: 'project'` when the change applies at once, or `kind: 'preparation'` while the new provider's image builds. |

See [Computers](/docs/guide#computers).

### The session handle

`kortix.session(projectId, sessionId)` or `p.session(sessionId)` returns `s`. The `session_id` is also the sandbox id. Methods that reach the runtime call `ensureReady()` first. The guide is [Sessions from code](/docs/developers#sessions-from-code).

| Area | Method | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Row | `get({ showErrors? }?)` | The session row. |
| Row | `update({ name?, labels?, metadata? })` | Changes the name, labels and metadata. |
| Row | `delete()` · `restore()` | Delete · restore a deleted session inside its window (`restore_until`). |
| Row | `setPinned(pinned)` · `setArchived(archived)` | The caller's own pin and archive. Pinning removes the archive; archiving removes the pin. No one else's list changes. Needs a person's credential; an agent token gets `403`. |
| Lifecycle | `start(…)` · `startOrThrow(…)` | Start the runtime; a failed request yields `null` · the same, but a failed request rejects with the `ApiError` (`status`, `code`, and a `402` billing `detail`). |
| Lifecycle | `ensureReady({ readyTimeoutMs?, session? }?)` | Provisions or resumes the sandbox and waits until it is ready. Default `readyTimeoutMs`: `180_000`. Idempotent. |
| Lifecycle | `restart()` · `stop()` | Restart the runtime · stop it. Both reset the handle's readiness. |
| Lifecycle | `recover()` | Continues a lost session on a new computer. A session is lost when the provider removed its sandbox (`start` fails with reason `runtime_identity_unavailable`). Refusals: `409 session_not_lost`, `409 session_transcript_missing`, `409 session_sandbox_removal_pending` (retry in a minute), `503 session_log_restore_off`. |
| Lifecycle | `health(init?)` | The runtime probe, including `capabilities`. Never throws on a non-2xx status. |
| Prompts | `send(text, { model?, agent? }?)` | Queues a prompt. `model` is `{ providerID, modelID }`. Resolves when the prompt is durable, not when the turn ends. |
| Prompts | `abort()` | Stops the current turn. |
| Prompts | `prompts.create(input)` · `.list()` · `.remove(promptId)` · `.retry(promptId)` · `.edit(promptId, text)` · `.interrupt(promptId)` · `.hold(held)` | The prompt inbox. |
| Events | `stream({ onEvent, onGapRehydrate?, signal? })` | Opens the live event stream. Resolves to a handle with `close()`. Options: [openEventStream](#event-stream). |
| Conversation | `messages({ conversationId?, limit?, before?, signal? }?)` | One page of the transcript (`kortix.transcript.v1`). `conversationId` reads a sub-agent conversation. |
| Conversation | `pending()` | Conversation statuses and the permission requests and questions that wait for an answer. |
| Conversation | `answerPermission(requestId, 'once' \| 'always' \| 'reject', message?)` | Answers a permission request. |
| Conversation | `answerQuestion(requestId, answers \| null)` | Answers a question, one answer per question, or dismisses it with `null`. |
| Conversation | `compact({ providerID, modelID }?)` | Summarizes the context. Needs the `session.compact` capability. |
| Conversation | `rewind(messageId)` · `restoreRewind()` | Rewind to a message · undo the rewind. Needs the `session.rewind` capability; pi does not serve it. |
| Stored reads | `turn()` | The current turn, read on the server. |
| Stored reads | `log({ thread?, before_seq?, limit? }?)` | One page of the stored session log, or `null` when the session has none. It answers while the sandbox is stopped. |
| Stored reads | `messageAuthors()` · `modelUsage()` · `cost()` · `audit(limit?, options?)` | Who wrote each message · model usage · the session's usage record · the session's audit actions. |
| Model and agent | `setModel({ providerID, modelID } \| undefined)` · `setAgent(agent)` | The model or agent of every later `send` on this handle. `send(text, { model, agent })` overrides it for one prompt. |
| Model and agent | `changeModel('provider/model')` | Persists the model and restarts the runtime. |
| Scope | `scope()` · `rescope(scope)` | Read · change the session's scope. |
| Config | `configState()` · `reloadConfig(input?)` · `reloadConfigStream(…)` | Whether the session runs the config the manifest compiles to · reload it · reload with progress. |
| Files | `files.list(dir)` · `.read(path)` · `.readBlob(path)` · `.status()` · `.findFiles(query, { type?, limit? })` · `.findText(pattern)` | Read the sandbox file system. |
| Files | `files.upload(file, …)` · `.write(path, blob)` · `.create(path)` · `.copy(from, to)` · `.remove(path)` · `.mkdir(dir)` · `.rename(from, to)` | Write the sandbox file system. Behavior: [Files](#files). |
| Files | `attachments.upload(file, options?)` · `attachments.read(attachmentId, signal?)` | Session attachments. `read` returns a `Blob` and works while the sandbox is stopped. |
| Previews | `previews()` · `previewUrl(port, path = '/')` · `proxyUrl(url?)` · `sandboxPortUrl(port)` | Preview candidates · a reachable URL for a sandbox port · a proxy URL for an agent's `localhost` URL · the raw port URL. The last three throw `SessionNotReadyError` before `ensureReady()`. |
| People | `participants()` · `setSharing(intent)` | The session's people · who can open it. `intent` is `{ mode: 'private', ownerId }`, `{ mode: 'project' }` or `{ mode: 'members', memberIds?, groupIds? }`. Owner or project manager only. |
| People | `presence({ tab_id, active, alerts? }, { keepalive? }?)` | This tab's presence lease. Returns `{ ok, awake_until, awake_cap_at }`. |
| People | `publicShares.list()` · `.create(…)` · `.revoke(…)` | Public share links for a preview or a workspace file. A `{ transcript: true }` body returns `400`. |
| Git | `commit(input?)` | Commits and pushes the sandbox's pending changes to the session branch. |
| Reminders | `reminders.list()` · `.create(input)` · `.update(reminderId, input)` · `.remove(reminderId)` · `.run(reminderId)` | The session's reminders. `run` fires one now; the schedule does not move. |

`s.transcript()`, `s.transcriptSync()` and `s.runtime` are deprecated. See [Deprecated names](#deprecated-names).

### React hooks

Every hook is in `@kortix/sdk/react`. Most are React Query hooks: unless a row says otherwise, a hook returns the query result (`data`, `isLoading`, `error`, …) plus the mutations the row names. A write refreshes the matching reads. Set up the providers as in [React > Set up](/docs/developers#set-up).

#### Session hooks

| Hook | What it returns |
| --- | --- |
| `useSession(projectId, sessionId, options?)` | The whole session lifecycle: start, readiness, live stream, messages and actions. Tables: [React > useSession](/docs/developers#usesession). |
| `useSessionSend(sessionId, options?)` | A send function with `KortixSendError` classification. See [Send and handle errors](/docs/developers#send-and-handle-errors). |
| `useSessionPrompts(projectId, sessionId, { pollMs?, enabled? }?)` | The prompt inbox of one session. |
| `useSessionWorking(projectId, sessionId, { enabled?, runtimeSessionId? }?)` | Whether a turn is in progress, and why it ended. |
| `useRuntimeSupports(capability)` | `boolean`: whether the open session's runtime serves a capability. `true` until the runtime first answers. |
| `useSessionMessages(session, { throttleMs? }?)` | The messages of one conversation as `MessageWithParts[]`. |
| `useSessionLog(session)` · `useSessionLogThread(session, threadId?, { throttleMs? }?)` | The session log, or `null` · one thread of it. |
| `useChatTurns(messages)` | `TurnView[]`: each message classified with `classifyTurn`. |
| `useSessionChildren(projectId, parentSessionId, { q, limit, enabled })` | The sessions one parent started, paged. |
| `useSessionReminders(projectId, sessionId)` | Reminders, with `update` and `remove`. |

#### Project and account hooks

| Hook | What it returns |
| --- | --- |
| `useAccounts({ userId, enabled })` | The caller's accounts. Pass the signed-in person's `userId` in a host where the person can change without a reload. |
| `useProjects(accountId?, options?)` | The projects of one account. |
| `useProjectSessions(projectId, options)` | One project's sessions, paged, newest activity first. Read `sessions` (flat) and `fetchNextPage`. Filters as in [Sessions](#sessions). Adds `keepPreviousData` (keep the old rows while a new filter loads), `refetchInterval` and `pollFirstPageOnly` (experimental). |
| `useProjectModels(projectId)` | Selectable models as `FlatModel[]`. Empty while the list loads. |
| `useProjectModelPickerCatalog(projectId)` | The model-picker record per model (`reasoning_options`, `temperature`, `limit`). |
| `useModelAccess(projectId)` | Disabled providers and models, and `setEnabled({ target, id, enabled })`. |
| `useCustomLlmProviders(projectId)` | `{ providers, isLoading, error, save(providerId, input), remove(providerId), isSaving }`. |
| `useProjectConfig(projectId)` | The config summary (default agent, slash commands) as `ProjectConfigSummary`. |
| `useVisibleAgents({ projectId })` | The agents a person can pick, as `Agent[]`. |
| `useProjectSecrets(projectId)` | Secrets, with `upsert`, `remove`, `setPersonal` and `removePersonal`. |
| `useAccountSecretResources(accountId, projectId?)` | Account secret resources. |
| `useProjectTriggers(projectId)` | Triggers, with `create`, `update`, `remove`, `fire`, `dryRun` and `replay`. `fire.mutate(slug)` or `fire.mutate({ slug, payload? })` returns `FireProjectTriggerResponse`. `dryRun.mutate(slug \| { slug, payload? })` returns `ProjectTriggerDryRunResult` and writes nothing. `replay.mutate({ slug, runId, confirmUnverified? })`. A fire and a replay refresh the triggers and every run list. |
| `useTriggerRuns(projectId, slug, { enabled?, refetchInterval?, limit? }?)` · `useProjectTriggerRuns(projectId, { status? }?, { enabled?, refetchInterval? }?)` | Runs as an infinite query paged by `next_cursor`. Read `data.pages[].runs`; call `fetchNextPage()` while `hasNextPage`. No polling unless you pass `refetchInterval` (ms). `limit` is 1 to 100 (server default 25), and a set limit is its own cache entry. |
| `useProjectReminders(projectId)` | Reminders, with `update`, `remove`, `updateMany` and `removeMany`. |
| `useChangeRequests(projectId, status?)` | Change requests, with `open`, `merge`, `close` and `requestChanges`. |
| `useReviewItemPages(projectId, query)` · `useReviewSummary(projectId)` · `useSessionsNeedingInput(projectId)` | The review inbox as an infinite query (rows: `reviewItemsOf(query.data)`) · the summary, polled every 60 s · blocked sessions, polled every 30 s. |
| `useProjectApps(projectId)` | Apps, with `create`, `update`, `start`, `stop` and `remove`. |
| `useAppDeployments` · `useAppDeployment` | Deployments with `deploy` and `rollback` · one deployment with its build log. |
| `useAppMachines(projectId, appId)` · `useAppVolumes(projectId, appId)` · `useAppVolumeSnapshots(projectId, appId, volumeId)` · `useAppAccess` | Machines with `start`, `stop`, `restart`, `destroy`, `scale`, polled every 5 s · volumes with `snapshot`, `restore`, `fork`, `extend`, `destroy` · snapshots, newest first · `{ policy, session, update }`. |
| `useKortixAppViewer()` | Inside an App: `{ status: 'loading' \| 'anonymous' \| 'viewer', viewer }`. |
| `useConnectorQuery(projectId, slug, action, args, { account, enabled, staleTime })` | One connector action's output as a cached query. Use it only for read actions: every fetch runs the action. |
| `useConnectorSetup(token, { backendUrl, storage, openPopup, onOpened })` | The flow behind a connect link (`/connect/<token>`): `phase` (`loading`, `ready`, `starting`, `opened`, `connected`, `error`), `info`, `error`, `connectedAs`, `connect()`. You supply the popup opener. |
| `useFeatureFlag(projectId, key)` | `{ enabled, isLoading }`. Loading and errors read `false`. |
| `useCan(target, action)` · `useCans(target, actions)` | `{ allowed, reason, isLoading, isError }` · one request for several actions, a map keyed by action (keep `actions` stable across renders). `target` is `{ accountId }`, `{ accountId, resource }` or `{ projectId }`. Loading and errors read `allowed: false`. |
| `usePermissionsFor(accountId, memberUserId, probes)` | What another member can do. Needs `member.read`. |
| `useSetBillingPlan()` | A mutation over `billing.setPlan`. |
| `useGatewayOverview` · `useGatewaySeries` · `useGatewayBreakdown` · `useGatewaySessions` · `useGatewayErrors` | Gateway usage over `days` (default 30). |
| `useGatewayLogs` · `useGatewayLog` · `useGatewayBudgets` · `useSetGatewayBudget` · `useDeleteGatewayBudget` · `useGatewayKeys` · `useCreateGatewayKey` · `useRevokeGatewayKey` · `useGatewayRoutingPolicy(projectId)` | Gateway logs, budgets, keys and routing policy. |
| `useCaptureWorkspace` · `useSetCaptureEnabled` · `useCaptureDevices` · `useRevokeCaptureDevice` · `useCapturePolicy` · `useSetCapturePolicy` · `useCaptureDays` · `useCaptureTimeline` · `useCaptureTimelineItems` · `useCaptureSearch` · `useCaptureChunkMedia` · `useCaptureDeviceGrant` · `useApproveCaptureDevice` · `useDenyCaptureDevice` | Kortix Capture. Each account hook takes the account id first. A `null` id sends no request. `useCaptureDevices` polls every 10 s. |

Rules for several hooks:

- `useCan` and its siblings decide what to show. The API checks every write again. Probe the action the route checks, for example `PROJECT_ACTIONS.PROJECT_GITOPS_PUSH` (`project.gitops.push`), never a role. After a write that changes access, call `invalidatePermissionProbes(queryClient, { accountId, userId })`. A verdict stays cached for 5 minutes otherwise.
- After a runtime restart or a config reload, call `resetRuntimeQueries(queryClient)`.

#### Query keys and cache helpers

| Name | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `qk` | The query key factory of every hook. Families: `qk.accounts`, `qk.projects`, `qk.project`, `qk.notifications`, `qk.drives`, `qk.capture`. Each family except `qk.capture` has `scope(…)`, the prefix of all its keys; `qk.capture.account(accountId)` is the prefix of one account's Capture keys. Example: `qk.project.detail(projectId)`, `qk.project.secrets(projectId)`. |
| `invalidateProject(queryClient, projectId)` | Invalidates every query under `qk.project.scope(projectId)`. |
| `invalidatePermissionProbes(queryClient, { accountId?, userId? })` | Invalidates cached `useCan` verdicts. |
| `resetRuntimeQueries(queryClient)` | Removes every runtime query, so the next read comes from the new runtime. |
| `resetIdentityState(…)` | Clears per-person state on sign-out. See [Sign-out and identity changes](/docs/developers#sign-out-and-identity-changes). |

