```tsx
import { useQueryClient } from '@tanstack/react-query';
import { qk } from '@kortix/sdk/react';

function useRefreshSecrets(projectId: string) {
  const queryClient = useQueryClient();
  return () => queryClient.invalidateQueries({ queryKey: qk.project.secrets(projectId) });
}
```

### Framework-free modules

The root `@kortix/sdk` exports these modules next to the client.

| Module | Names | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| Files | `files`, `FileExistsError`, `uploadNativeFile` | File operations on the process-wide active sandbox, with the methods of `s.files`. A host with more than one session uses `s.files`. |
| Session health | `getSessionHealth(runtimeUrl?, init?)`, `isRuntimeReady(health)`, `runtimeSupports(capabilities, name)` | Probe a runtime and read its capabilities. |
| Preview URLs | `detectLocalhostUrls`, `rewriteLocalhostUrl`, `proxyLocalhostUrl`, `parseLocalhostUrl`, `buildWebProxyUrl` | Rewrite an agent's `localhost` URLs into reachable proxy URLs. |
| Event stream | `openEventStream`, `narrowChatEvent`, `heartbeatGapEvent` | The live stream behind `s.stream`, and a narrower for chat events. |
| Auth | `authenticatedFetch`, `getAuthToken` | A `fetch` that adds the token from your `getToken`. |
| API client | `backendApi` | The typed HTTP client under every method. Use it only for a route with no method. |
| Errors | `ApiError`, `AuthError`, `BillingError`, `RequestTooLargeError`, `isAuthFailure`, `isFeatureDisabledError`, `featureDisabledKey`, `FEATURE_DISABLED_CODE`, `REPOSITORY_DISCONNECTED_CODE` | Error types and checks. Catalog: [Errors and retries](/docs/developers#errors-and-retries). |
| Classify | `classifyPart`, `classifyTurn`, `toolViewModel`, `toolInfo`, `humanizeToolName`, `toToolView`, `sessionLogTurns` and the other turn helpers | Turn session messages and session-log blocks into renderable parts. Guide: [Render messages yourself](/docs/developers#render-messages-yourself). |
| Composer lists | `projectConfigAgentsToRuntimeAgents`, `composerSelectableAgents`, `resolveComposerAgent`, `pickerProviderList`, `flattenModels`, `createModelVisibility`, `modelInDefaultView`, `resolveComposerModel`, `resolveModelDefault`, `sessionModelCandidates`, `latestPromptModel`, `modelRefToKey` | Build an agent picker and a model picker with the rules of the Kortix web app. Every function is pure. |
| Transcript export | `formatTranscript(session, messages, options?)` | One session as a Markdown string. |
| Message ids | `mintWireMessageId()`, `WIRE_MESSAGE_ID` | Mint a message id for a prompt · validate one. Never mint an id by hand. |
| Sandboxes by id | `restartSandbox(id)`, `stopSandbox(id)`, `getSandboxById(id)` | Act on a session's sandbox with only its `session_id`. One `GET /sessions/:id` finds the project, however old the session is. A provider external id still works, but only for a session on its project's first sessions page. With the project id, use `s.restart()`, `s.stop()` and `s.get()`. |
| Sandboxes, every project | `getSandbox()`, `ensureSandbox()`, `listSandboxes(id?)` | Read the first sessions page of each project only (default 50 rows). For older sessions, use `p.sessions.listPage()`. |

#### Files

```ts
import { FileExistsError, files } from '@kortix/sdk';

export async function saveNotes(file: File): Promise<void> {
  const { content } = await files.read('/workspace/README.md');
  console.log(content.length);

  await files.upload(file, '/workspace/uploads', file.name, {
    onProgress: ({ loadedBytes, totalBytes }) => console.log(loadedBytes, '/', totalBytes),
  });

  try {
    await files.create('/workspace/notes.md');
  } catch (error) {
    if (error instanceof FileExistsError) console.log('exists:', error.path);
    else throw error;
  }
}
```

| Method | Behavior |
| --- | --- |
| `upload(file, dir, name, { onProgress? })` | A file of 8 MiB or less goes in one request. A larger file goes in 8 MiB chunks, each checked on arrival. `onProgress` runs after each chunk. |
| `write(path, blob)` | Replaces the file at `path`. Fails when `path` is a folder. |
| `create(path)` | Makes an empty file and never overwrites. Throws `FileExistsError` (`status` `409`, `code` `FILE_EXISTS`) when anything exists at `path`. |
| `uploadNativeFile({ uri, name, type? }, targetPath?, baseUrl?)` | Uploads a React Native device file in one request. The bytes stream from `uri` and never enter the JS heap. |

#### Session health

`getSessionHealth(runtimeUrl?, init?)` sends `GET {runtimeUrl}/kortix/health`. It never throws on a non-2xx status. It returns `{ status, ok, health, body, hop, upstreamStatus }`. Without a runtime URL it returns `status: 0` and `ok: false`. `isRuntimeReady(health)` is `true` when the runtime accepts work. `s.health()` is the same probe for one session.

#### Event stream

`openEventStream(options)` returns `{ close() }` at once. `s.stream(options)` calls it with the session's runtime URL after `ensureReady()`. Streams to one URL share one connection.

| Option | Default | What it does |
| --- | --- | --- |
| `url` | — | The session runtime: `{backendUrl}/p/{externalId}/{port}`. |
| `onEvent(event)` | — | Called once per event, in order, after coalescing. Consecutive deltas of one part, or of one session-log block, in the same 16 ms flush arrive as one event: `properties.delta` is their joined text and `coalesced` lists the replaced events. A throw in the handler is caught and logged. |
| `onGapRehydrate(gapMs)` | — | Called when a reconnect is established, with the gap since the last frame. It fires when the dropped stream had delivered events, or when the gap exceeds 5 s. |
| `signal` | — | Stops the stream when aborted, like `close()`. |
| `connectTimeoutMs` | `20000` | Maximum wait for the connect call before a retry. |
| `heartbeatTimeoutMs` | `60000` | Maximum quiet time on an open stream before it reconnects. |
| `maxConsecutiveHardFailures` | `8` | Consecutive hard failures before the stream parks. Backoff grows from 1 s to a 30 s cap. |
| `onParked({ consecutiveFailures, lastError })` | — | Called once when the stream parks. A parked handle never reconnects: open a new stream. |
| `onConnectionChange(state)` | — | `connecting`, `open` or `lost`. |

`narrowChatEvent(event)` narrows a `RuntimeEvent` to the `KortixChatEvent` union. The event list is in [Stream events](/docs/developers#stream-events).

#### Workspace search

`createWorkspaceSearchClient(fileClient, getCacheKey?)` from `@kortix/sdk/workspace-search` returns ranked file and folder search over a session workspace, with a cached index. `fileClient` has `listFiles(path)` and `findFiles(query, options?)`, the same shape as `s.files`. `getCacheKey` partitions the cache, for example by session id. The entry also exports the ranking helpers `rankWorkspaceSearchEntry`, `searchIndexedWorkspaceEntries`, `normalizeSearchQuery` and `normalizeWorkspacePath`.

### Session log types

The root exports the types of the session log, the runtime-neutral record of a session, and its constants. It exports no validators. `s.log(query)` reads one page. In React, `useSessionLog` and `useSessionLogThread` return it.

| Name | What it is |
| --- | --- |
| `SESSION_LOG_SCHEMA` · `SESSION_LOG_MINOR` | `'kortix.session/2'` · `1`. |
| `SessionLog` | The record: threads, todos, pending questions and permissions, `harness` (the runtime that writes it, and the history of runtimes), `restore_grade`. |
| `SessionLogThread` · `SessionLogMessage` | One thread (the main thread or a sub-agent) · one message with `seq`, `role`, `kind`, `status`, `model`, `usage`, `blocks`. |
| `SessionLogBlock` | The union of `TextBlock`, `ReasoningBlock`, `AttachmentBlock`, `ToolCallBlock`, `CompactionBlock`, `SubtaskBlock`, `StepBlock`, `HarnessBlock`, keyed by `type`. |
| `ToolResultContent`, `CompactionLayoutEntry`, `ThreadInteraction`, `ModelRef` | Parts of the records above. |
| `KNOWN_TOOL_KINDS` · `KnownToolKind` · `SessionLogToolKind` | The tool kinds every reader renders: `shell`, `read`, `write`, `edit`, `patch`, `list`, `glob`, `grep`, `web_fetch`, `web_search`, `todo`, `task`, `question`, `plan`, `mcp`, `other` · a block's tool kind, any string. Render an unknown kind as `other`. |
| `SessionLogTodo` | One todo item. |
| `RestoreGrade` · `GradeCounts` | How completely the record restores (`native`, `converted` or `partial`) · what is missing. |
| `SessionLogPage` · `SessionLogPageQuery` | The answer of `s.log()` · its query `{ thread?, before_seq?, limit? }`. |

A text over 256 kB is stored by reference, in a `TextBlock` and in a `text` item of a tool result. Its `text` holds the first 16 kB, `bytes` holds the UTF-8 length of the full text, and `ref` is `kortix-attachment://<project_id>/<session_id>/<attachment_id>`. `s.attachments.read(attachmentId)` returns the full text as a `Blob`, also while the sandbox is stopped.

```ts
import type { SessionLog, SessionLogBlock } from '@kortix/sdk';

export function toolCalls(log: SessionLog): SessionLogBlock[] {
  return log.threads
    .flatMap((thread) => thread.messages.flatMap((message) => message.blocks))
    .filter((block) => block.type === 'tool_call');
}
```

A text over 256 kB is offered whole with the full-text helpers below. `fetchSessionAttachment(ref)` reads one by its `ref`.

#### Session log helpers

Pure functions over session-log blocks, from the root `@kortix/sdk`. They have no React and no DOM. The guide is [React > Render blocks from the session log](/docs/developers#render-blocks-from-the-session-log).

| Name | What it does |
| --- | --- |
| `sessionLogTurns(thread, options?)` | One `SessionLogTurn` per user message: `id`, `user`, `assistant` (the replies, by `reply_to` then order), `partial` (the prompt is not loaded), `blocks`, `status`, `error` (`code`, `message`, `details`, `raw`), `cost` (the replies' usage priced with the platform markup), `compaction` (`block`, `hasContent`, `inFlight`), `threads` (the sub-threads it spawned), `plan`, `shell` (the bash call of a shell turn, which needs `session.shell`), `response` (the last text block), `attachments`, `live`, `queued`, `retrying`. Options: `busy`, `liveMessageId`, `held` (the prompts the inbox holds, in queue order), `threads`, `pricing`, `hideOutOfContext`. `hideOutOfContext: true` leaves out every message with `in_context: false` that is not streaming. A turn keeps its identity while its messages and derived fields do. |
| `sessionLogTurnSegments(blocks, options?)` | The segments of a turn: `burst` (a run of non-text blocks), `text`, `standalone` (a `show` or sub-agent call, or a call named in `standaloneCallIds`), `show-group` (adjacent `show` calls). |
| `splitSessionLogTurnWork(segments, { working })` | The settled-turn fold: `work`, `hoisted` (the `show` deliverables), `final` (the last text). `null` while the turn works or has no work. |
| `sessionLogBurstSteps(blocks)` | The rows of a burst: `thought` (a run of reasoning), `block`, `group` (same-family calls, with `family`, `label`, `status`). An answered question is its own row. |
| `sessionLogBurstSummary(blocks)` | A burst's step counts: `total` (a run of reasoning is one), `failed`, `completed`, `hasPlumbing`. |
| `sessionLogBurstRunning(blocks, { working, trailing? })` | Whether the burst is in progress. Never once the turn stops working. |
| `sessionLogShowGroupCall(calls)` | A `show-group` segment as one `show` call: `input.items` is the carousel; a failed call keeps its slot as an `error` item. |
| `sessionLogAnsweredQuestionCall(call, answers, input?)` | A `question` call shown as answered before the runtime confirms the answers. |
| `sessionLogRetrying(thread)` | Whether the thread's last reply is open with a retryable provider error, read without grouping turns. Pass it into `busy`. |
| `sessionLogActivityText(turn, childMessages?)` | The activity line of a live turn, for example `Reading files...`. A running task reads its sub-thread's messages. |
| `sessionLogThreadMetrics(thread, options?)` | One thread's `cost`, `messages` (`all`, `user`, `assistant`), `tokens` (`input`, `output`, `reasoning`, `cacheRead`, `cacheWrite`) and `context` (the last reply with tokens: its `usage` is the context size). Options: `pricing`. |
| `classifySessionLogMessage(message)` | `classifyTurn` of one session-log message: its blocks as `ClassifiedPart`s and its error as `TurnError`. For a renderer that takes `ClassifiedPart`s. |
| `sessionLogPromptMessage(prompt, threadId)` | The user message of a queued inbox prompt the thread has not placed: id `message_id`, else `queued-<prompt_id>`. Append it after the thread's messages and name it in `held`. |
| `isSessionLogRestoreNote(message)` | Whether the message is the note a partial restore leaves for the agent. Render it as a notice, not as a person's prompt. |
| `sessionLogRestoreNotice(session)` | What a partial restore could not bring back: `{ generation, missing }`, `missing` a list of `{ kind, count }` (`attachment`, `text`, `tool_input`, `tool_result`, `cut_point`, `other`). `null` unless the grade is `partial` and a restore wrote the record. Pass `useSessionLogRecord(session)`. |
| `sessionLogReferencedTexts(messages)` | The texts stored by reference that a transcript renders, once per `ref`: `{ ref, bytes }`. |
| `sessionLogWithFullTexts(thread, texts)` | `thread` with each text whose `ref` is a key of `texts` replaced by the whole text. |
| `sessionLogFullTextAction(refs)` | `'inline'` up to `SESSION_LOG_FULL_TEXT_INLINE_MAX_BYTES` (1 MiB) in all, else `'download'`. |
| `sessionLogFullTextPlan(refs)` | `{ inline, download }`: the smallest texts fill the 1 MiB inline budget first, the rest download. |
| `toolCallMetadata(call)` | Transitional. The runtime's display metadata of a tool call, for example bash `command` or task `sessionId`. One shared empty object when there is none. |

`toToolView`, `getChildSessionId`, `partOutcome`, `contextToolTrigger` and `shouldShowToolPart` accept a `ToolCallBlock` as well as a v1 tool part. `freshSendHint` and `fallbackBusyRowAfterTurnId` accept a `SessionLogTurn[]` as well as v1 turns, and `stepLabel` accepts any session-log block (a non-call block reads by its `type`). The closure rule: a call closed by the runtime (`result.synthetic`, or `result.error.code` `'interrupted'`) renders `running` or `pending` on a live turn and `interrupted` on any other.

```ts
import { sessionLogCallStatus, sessionLogTurnSegments, sessionLogTurns, type SessionLogThread } from '@kortix/sdk';

export function standaloneCalls(thread: SessionLogThread, busy: boolean) {
  return sessionLogTurns(thread, { busy }).flatMap((turn) =>
    sessionLogTurnSegments(turn.blocks)
      .filter((segment) => segment.kind === 'standalone')
      .map((segment) => ({ segment, status: sessionLogCallStatus(segment.block, turn.live) })),
  );
}
```

#### v1 turn helpers

Pure functions over v1 messages (`MessageWithParts`, `kortix.transcript.v1`, `KORTIX_TRANSCRIPT_SCHEMA`), the shape that `s.messages()` and `useSessionMessages` return.

| Names | What they do |
| --- | --- |
| `groupMessagesIntoTurns(messages)` · `collectTurnParts(turn)` | Pair each user message with the assistant messages that answered it · all parts of one turn. |
| `findLastTextPart(parts)` · `turnHasSteps(parts)` | The final answer text · whether the turn has tool, compaction, snapshot or patch parts. |
| `getTurnStatus(parts, childMessages?)` · `getWorkingState(…)` · `formatDuration(ms)` | A status line such as `Reading files...`. Pass `childMessages` to show a sub-agent's activity. |
| `getTurnError(turn)` · `getChildSessionError(messages)` · `unwrapError(error)` | Turn and sub-agent errors, normalized. |
| `getRetryInfo(status)` · `getRetryMessage(status)` | Retry state `{ attempt, message, next, details? }` · the full retry message. |
| `getChildSessionId(part)` · `getChildSessionToolParts(messages)` · `rootRuntimeSession` · `directSubsessions` · `runtimeSessionsOf` · `projectSessionForRuntimeId` | Connect a delegating tool call to its child conversation. |
| `getHiddenToolParts(…)` · `isToolPartHidden(…)` | Which tool parts a transcript hides. |
| `isTextPart`, `isToolPart`, `isReasoningPart`, `isFilePart`, `isAgentPart`, `isCompactionPart`, `isSnapshotPart`, `isPatchPart`, `isStepPart`, `getPartText` | Type guards. |
| `sortSessions(now)` · `childMapByParent(sessions)` · `allDescendantIds(map, id)` | Session list order and hierarchy. |
| `getFilename`, `getDirectory`, `relativizePath`, `stripAnsi`, `formatTokens` | Formatting. |

The grouping and status functions accept the structural types `PartLike`, `MessageInfoLike`, `TurnLike`, `ToolStateLike` and `SessionStatusLike`, so your own message shapes work.

### Deprecated names

Each name below carries `@deprecated` and resolves until the next major version. Your editor flags each use. The list is not complete: your editor flags every other deprecated name too, and its `@deprecated` tag names the replacement when one exists.

#### Subpaths

These subpaths re-export names that the root or `@kortix/sdk/react` already exports, or wrap internal stores.

| Subpaths | Use instead |
| --- | --- |
| `/config`, `/auth`, `/api-client`, `/projects-client`, `/feature-flags`, `/fresh-sessions`, `/instance-routes`, `/platform-client`, `/event-stream`, `/files`, `/session`, `/session/url`, `/turns` | The same names from the root `@kortix/sdk`. |
| `/sync-store`, `/server-store`, `/sandbox-connection-store`, `/idb-sync-cache`, `/internal/sync-store`, `/internal/server-store`, `/internal/sandbox-connection-store` | No replacement. Read session state with `useSession`. |
| `/message-queue` | `s.send`, or `useSession().send`. Kortix orders prompts on the server. |

#### Names

| Deprecated | Use instead |
| --- | --- |
| `claimNext`, `completeInFlight`, `createSessionQueue`, `editQueued`, `enqueue`, `failInFlight`, `removeQueued`, `reorderQueued`, `retryFailed`, `QueuedMessage`, `QueuedMessageInput`, `SessionQueue` | `s.send`, or `useSession().send`. |
| `wireIdClock`, `wireIdClockAt`, `wireIdClockDelta`, `maxWireIdClock`, `isWireIdAheadOf`, `newestWireIdClock`, `mintWireMessageIdAbove`, `MintWireMessageIdAboveInput`, `MintedWireMessageId`, `WIRE_ID_TIME_SCALE`, `WIRE_ID_TIME_MASK`, `WIRE_ID_CLOCK_TOLERANCE`, `WIRE_ID_BACKDATE_MS` | `mintWireMessageId` and `WIRE_MESSAGE_ID`, which stay current. Message ids are opaque. |
| `s.runtime` | The session methods: `messages`, `pending`, `answerPermission`, `answerQuestion`, `compact`, `send`, `abort`, `rewind`. |
| `s.transcript()` · `s.transcriptSync()` | `s.log(query)`. |
| `findActiveTranscriptShare`, `getPublicSessionShare`, `getPublicSessionShareMessages`, `PublicSessionShareError`, `PublicSessionShareMeta`, `PublicSessionTranscript`, `PublicSessionTranscriptMessage`, `PublicSessionTranscriptToolCall` | No replacement. The API removed public transcript sharing; every call returns `404`. |
| `clientSource` (a `createKortix` option) | Remove it. It is inert. Send `clientVersion` to identify a release. |
| `p.updateExperimentalFeature` · `updateExperimentalFeature` · `ExperimentalFeatureKey` · `ExperimentalFeatureView` | `p.updateFeatureFlag` · `updateFeatureFlag` · `FeatureFlagKey` · `FeatureFlagView` |
| `p.connectors.setAuthorizationStrategy` · `setConnectorAuthorizationStrategy` | Remove the call. It changes nothing. |
| `getConnectionPolicies` · `setConnectionPolicies` | `p.connectors.policies.get` · `p.connectors.policies.set` |
| `pipedreamConnect` · `pipedreamFinalize` · `p.connectors.connections.pipedreamConnect` · `.pipedreamFinalize` | `connectorConnect` · `connectorFinalize` |
| `listDiscoverIntegrations` · `getDiscoverIntegration` · `DiscoverIntegration*` types | `listDiscoverConnectors` · `getDiscoverConnector` · `DiscoverConnector*` types |
| `SessionScopeResult` · `toWorkspaceRelative` | `SessionScope` · `toDaemonPath` |
| `setCachedAuthToken` · `setBootstrapAuthToken` | Return the token from your own `getToken`. |
| `billing.checkout.createSession` · `billing.checkout.confirmSession` · `createPerSeatCheckout` · `claimPerSeatBilling` | `billing.setPlan(input)`. They reject with `ENDPOINT_RETIRED` and send no request. |
| `billing.subscription.scheduleDowngrade` · `.cancelScheduledChange` · `.prorationPreview` | `billing.setPlan(input)` or `billing.subscription.createPortalSession`. They reject with `ENDPOINT_RETIRED`. |

Other deprecated root names wrap routes that the API does not serve. They reject with `ENDPOINT_RETIRED`, resolve `null`, or return `404`, and have no replacement.
